Clause / section / page: I2.7.2 / Framing - Longitudinal local frames in side structures / 13
Source URL: https://iacs.org.uk/resolutions/unified-requirements/ur-i/ur-i2-rev4-cln

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-I2-035-20260915T095840217297Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The actual net effective shear area A_w is to comply with A_w >= A_L, where A_L = 100^2*(AF*PPF_s*P_avg)*0.5*b_1*a/(0.577*sigma_y) [cm^2], b_1 = k_o*b_2, k_o = 1 - 0.3/b_prime, b_prime = b/s, and b_2 = b*(1 - 0.25*b_prime) when b_prime < 2, otherwise b_2 = s.
Normalized requirement:
The actual net effective shear area A_w shall satisfy A_w >= A_L, where A_L = 100^2*(AF*PPF_s*P_avg)*0.5*b_1*a/(0.577*sigma_y) [cm^2], b_1 = k_o*b_2, k_o = 1 - 0.3/b_prime, b_prime = b/s, and b_2 = b*(1 - 0.25*b_prime) when b_prime < 2, otherwise b_2 = s.
Required inputs: yieldStrength_MPa, shearAreaA

```turtle
gen:I2_035_StructuralMemberShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameSpacing ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:selectedHullAreaFactor ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:requiredLongitudinalFrameShearArea ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:CentiM2 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Required longitudinal frame shear area must match the declared formula within tolerance and actual shear area must be at least that value." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:frameSpacing/qudt:numericValue ?s ;
                      nltl:selectedHullAreaFactor/qudt:numericValue ?af ;
                      nltl:requiredLongitudinalFrameShearArea/qudt:numericValue ?required .

                ?ship nltl:hasStructuralMember $this ;
                      nltl:averageIcePressure/qudt:numericValue ?pavg ;
                      nltl:frameSpan/qudt:numericValue ?b ;
                      nltl:loadPatchHeight/qudt:numericValue ?a ;
                      nltl:peakPressureFactor/qudt:numericValue ?ppf ;
                      nltl:yieldStrength/qudt:numericValue ?sigma ;
                      nltl:shearArea/qudt:numericValue ?actual .

                BIND (?b / ?s AS ?bprime)
                BIND (IF(?bprime < 2, ?b * (1 - 0.25 * ?bprime), ?s) AS ?b2)
                BIND (1 - 0.3 / ?bprime AS ?ko)
                BIND (?ko * ?b2 AS ?b1)
                BIND (10000 * ?af * ?ppf * ?pavg * 0.5 * ?b1 * ?a / (0.577 * ?sigma) AS ?expected)

                FILTER (
                    ABS(?required - ?expected) > (1e-6 * ABS(?expected))
                    && ABS(?required - ?expected) > 1e-9
                    || ?actual < ?required
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

The shafting and stern tubes of side propellers shall normally be enclosed within plated bossings. If detached struts are used, due consideration shall be taken of their design, strength and attachments to the hull.

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF064ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasSidePropellerShafting ;
        sh:class nltl:sidePropellerShaftingAssembly ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship shall identify its side propeller shafting." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasSternTube ;
        sh:class nltl:sternTube ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship shall identify its stern tube." ;
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:not [
                sh:property [
                    sh:path nltl:hasDetachedStrut ;
                    sh:minCount 1 ;
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path ( nltl:hasSidePropellerShafting nltl:enclosedByPlatedBossing ) ;
                sh:class nltl:platedBossingStructure ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Side propeller shafting shall be enclosed within plated bossing when detached struts are not used." ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path ( nltl:hasSternTube nltl:enclosedByPlatedBossing ) ;
                sh:class nltl:platedBossingStructure ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The stern tube shall be enclosed within plated bossing when detached struts are not used." ;
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasDetachedStrut ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasDetachedStrut ;
                sh:node gen:TRF064DetachedStrutShape ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "When detached struts are used, design, strength, and hull-attachment evidence shall be provided." ;
            ]
        ]
    ) .

gen:TRF064DetachedStrutShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasStrutDesignEvidence ;
        sh:class nltl:strutDesignEvidence ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Detached strut design evidence is required." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasStrutStrengthEvidence ;
```

```turtle
                sh:message "The detached-strut applicability value must be false for the plated-bossing case."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasSidePropellerShafting ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:enclosedByPlatedBossing ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:class nltl:platedBossingStructure ;
                        sh:message "Each side-propeller shafting assembly must be enclosed within plated bossing."
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasSternTube ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:enclosedByPlatedBossing ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:class nltl:platedBossingStructure ;
                        sh:message "Each side-propeller stern tube must be enclosed within plated bossing."
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:detachedStrut ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "The detached-strut applicability value must be true when detached struts are used."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasDetachedStrut ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:class nltl:detachedStrutStructure ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:hasStrutDesignEvidence ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:class nltl:strutDesignEvidence ;
                        sh:message "Detached struts require design evidence."
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:hasStrutStrengthEvidence ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;

                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:class nltl:strutHullAttachmentEvidence ;
                        sh:message "Detached struts require hull-attachment evidence."
```

REQUIREMENT 043: TRF-059
====================================================================================================

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 43
Batch: BATCH_05
Requirement ID: TRF-059
Source family: TRAFICOM
Verification mode: DIRECT_CALCULATION
Source document: Ice Class Regulations and the Application Thereof
Source revision: TRAFICOM/68863/03.04.01.00/2021
Clause / section / page: 4.6.2 / Section modulus and shear area / 23
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-059-20260915T132250255473Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The effective shear area of a web frame shall be calculated using A = sqrt(3)*alpha*f_13*Q/sigma_y*10^4 [cm^2] (4.16), where Q is the maximum calculated shear force under the ice load F from 4.6.1, f_13 = 1.1, alpha is selected from Table 4-8, and sigma_y is the yield stress from 4.3.2. Table 4-8 (Af/Aw: alpha, gamma): 0:1.5,0; 0.2:1.23,0.44; 0.4:1.16,0.62; 0.6:1.11,0.71; 0.8:1.09,0.76; 1.0:1.07,0.80; 1.2:1.06,0.83; 1.4:1.05,0.85; 1.6:1.05,0.87; 1.8:1.04,0.88; 2.0:1.04,0.89.

Compute A_cm2 = sqrt(3)*alpha*1.1*Q_MN/sigma_y_N_mm2*10^4, where Q_MN is the maximum calculated shear force under F and alpha is selected from Table 4-8. @prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
```

```turtle
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF059WebFrameShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:webFrame ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumCalculatedShearForce ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloN ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:webFrameShearFactorAlpha ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:yieldStrength ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MegaPA ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:freeFlangeToWebAreaRatio ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:requiredShearArea ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:CentiM2 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "requiredShearArea must follow the Table 4-8 alpha selection and the declared shear-area formula within a relative tolerance of 1e-6." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:maximumCalculatedShearForce ?qNode ;
                      nltl:webFrameShearFactorAlpha ?alphaNode ;
                      nltl:yieldStrength ?sigmaNode ;
                      nltl:freeFlangeToWebAreaRatio ?ratioNode ;
                      nltl:requiredShearArea ?areaNode .

                ?qNode qudt:numericValue ?q .
                ?alphaNode qudt:numericValue ?alpha .
                ?sigmaNode qudt:numericValue ?sigma .
                ?ratioNode qudt:numericValue ?ratio .
                ?areaNode qudt:numericValue ?actual .

                FILTER (
                    (
                        ?ratio != 0.0 &&
                        ?ratio != 0.2 &&
                        ?ratio != 0.4 &&
                        ?ratio != 0.6 &&
                        ?ratio != 0.8 &&
                        ?ratio != 1.0 &&
                        ?ratio != 1.2 &&
                        ?ratio != 1.4 &&
                        ?ratio != 1.6 &&
                        ?ratio != 1.8 &&
                        ?ratio != 2.0
                    ) ||
                    (?ratio = 0.0 && ?alpha != 1.5) ||
                    (?ratio = 0.2 && ?alpha != 1.23) ||
                    (?ratio = 0.4 && ?alpha != 1.16) ||
                    (?ratio = 0.6 && ?alpha != 1.11) ||
                    (?ratio = 0.8 && ?alpha != 1.09) ||
                    (?ratio = 1.0 && ?alpha != 1.07) ||
                    (?ratio = 1.2 && ?alpha != 1.06) ||
                    (?ratio = 1.4 && ?alpha != 1.05) ||
                    (?ratio = 1.6 && ?alpha != 1.05) ||
                    (?ratio = 1.8 && ?alpha != 1.04) ||
                    (?ratio = 2.0 && ?alpha != 1.04)
                )

                BIND (
                    1.7320508075688772 * ?alpha * 1.1 *
                    (?q / 1000.0) / ?sigma * 10000.0
                    AS ?expected
                )

                FILTER (
                    ABS(?actual - ?expected) >
                    0.000001 *
                    IF(
                        ABS(?expected) > 0.000001,
                        ABS(?expected),
                        0.000001
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each web frame must provide exactly one free-flange-to-web area ratio as a dimensionless quantity." ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;

            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-059-WebFrameShearAreaShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:webFrame ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumCalculatedShearForce ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloN ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Maximum calculated shear force is required in kN."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:webFrameShearFactorAlpha ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Web frame shear factor alpha is required as a dimensionless quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:yieldStrength ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MegaPA ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Yield strength is required in MPa."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:freeFlangeToWebAreaRatio ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Free flange to web area ratio is required as a dimensionless quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:webFrameShearDistributionFactorF13 ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:minInclusive "1.1"^^xsd:decimal ;
                sh:maxInclusive "1.1"^^xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The web frame shear distribution factor f13 must equal 1.1."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:requiredShearArea ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:CentiM2 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Required shear area is required in cm2."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Required shear area must equal sqrt(3) times alpha times 1.1 times maximum shear force in MN divided by yield strength, converted to cm2, within a relative tolerance of 1e-6 and an absolute fallback of 1e-9." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:maximumCalculatedShearForce ?qForce ;
                      nltl:webFrameShearFactorAlpha ?qAlpha ;
                      nltl:yieldStrength ?qYield ;
                      nltl:webFrameShearDistributionFactorF13 ?qF13 ;
                      nltl:requiredShearArea ?qRequired .

                ?qForce qudt:numericValue ?force ;
                        qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> .
                ?qAlpha qudt:numericValue ?alpha ;
                        qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                ?qYield qudt:numericValue ?yield ;
                        qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA> .
                ?qF13 qudt:numericValue ?f13 ;
                      qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                ?qRequired qudt:numericValue ?required ;
                           qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/CentiM2> .

                FILTER (
                    ?required <
                    (
                        (1.7320508075688772 * ?alpha * ?f13 * (?force / 1000) / ?yield * 10000)
                        - (1e-6 * ABS(1.7320508075688772 * ?alpha * ?f13 * (?force / 1000) / ?yield * 10000) + 1e-9)
                    )
                    ||
                    ?required >
                    (
                        (1.7320508075688772 * ?alpha * ?f13 * (?force / 1000) / ?yield * 10000)
                        + (1e-6 * ABS(1.7320508075688772 * ?alpha * ?f13 * (?force / 1000) / ?yield * 10000) + 1e-9)
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The classification society rule length must be provided as a metre-valued quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [

        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:ruleLength ?ruleQuantity .
                ?ruleQuantity qudt:numericValue ?ruleValue .
                $this nltl:classificationSocietyRuleLength ?societyQuantity .
                ?societyQuantity qudt:numericValue ?societyValue .

            }
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-035-ShipRuleLengthShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:ruleLength ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue qudt:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The ship shall have one rule length quantity expressed in metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:classificationSocietyRuleLength ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue qudt:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The ship shall have one classification-society rule length quantity expressed in metres."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The ship's rule length shall equal the rule length used by the classification society." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:ruleLength ?ruleLengthQuantity .
                ?ruleLengthQuantity qudt:numericValue ?ruleLengthValue .
                $this nltl:classificationSocietyRuleLength ?societyRuleLengthQuantity .
                ?societyRuleLengthQuantity qudt:numericValue ?societyRuleLengthValue .
                FILTER (?ruleLengthValue != ?societyRuleLengthValue)
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
```

```turtle
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "directAnalysisUsed must be true for the direct-analysis branch."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:prescribedProcedureApplicability ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:message "Prescribed procedures must be marked not applicable when direct analysis is used."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:directAnalysisApprovalStatus ;
        sh:class nltl:evidenceState ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:message "Direct analysis must have approved status."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:structuralArrangement ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A structural arrangement is required when direct analysis is used."
    ] .

gen:TRF029DirectAnalysisNotUsedBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:directAnalysisUsed ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:message "directAnalysisUsed must be false for the prescribed-procedure branch."
    ] .
```

```turtle
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceClassDraught ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:brashIceResistanceCoefficientC1 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/N> ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:brashIceResistanceCoefficientC2 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/N> ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For an IA Super ship without a bulbous bow, breadth, length, and ice-class draught must be positive." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass nltl:iceClassIaSuper ;
                       nltl:bulbousBowPresent false ;
                       nltl:shipBreadth ?breadthNode ;
                       nltl:shipLength ?lengthNode ;
                       nltl:iceClassDraught ?draughtNode .
                ?breadthNode qudt:numericValue ?breadth .
                ?lengthNode qudt:numericValue ?length .
                ?draughtNode qudt:numericValue ?draught .
                FILTER (
                    ?breadth <= 0 ||
                    ?length <= 0 ||
                    ?draught <= 0
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Original source text:
For ships intended to operate in extended periods of darkness, searchlights suitable for continuous use to facilitate identification of ice shall be provided for each lifeboat.
Normalized requirement:
IF extended_darkness_operation = true, THEN every lifeboat shall have an assigned searchlight with continuous_use_suitability_status = approved.
Required inputs: Operating profile, lifeboat inventory, searchlight assignment, and suitability evidence

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-072-ShipShape> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:extendedDarknessOperation ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:message "The extended darkness operation applicability value must be false or true."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:and (
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:extendedDarknessOperation ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue true ;
                        sh:message "Extended darkness operation must be recorded as true for this requirement to apply."
                    ]
                ]
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:hasLifeboat ;
                        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-072-LifeboatShape>
                    ]
                ]
            )
        ]
    ) .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-072-LifeboatShape> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasAssignedSearchlight ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-072-SearchlightShape> ;
        sh:message "Each lifeboat must have an assigned searchlight when extended darkness operation applies."
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-072-SearchlightShape> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:searchlightContinuousUseSuitabilityStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The assigned searchlight must be suitable for continuous use."
    ] .
```

Original source text:
The acceptance criterion for designs is that the combined stresses from bending and shear, when using the von Mises yield criterion, are lower than the yield point sigmay. When the direct calculation is based on beam theory, the allowable shear stress must be no larger than 0.9*tauy, where tauy = sigmay/sqrt3.

```turtle
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

                BIND (?yield / 1.7320508075688772 AS ?expected)
                BIND (ABS(?shear - ?expected) AS ?difference)
                FILTER (
                    ?difference >
                    IF(
                        (ABS(?expected) * 0.000001) > 0.000000001,
                        (ABS(?expected) * 0.000001),

                    )
                )
```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:StressQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue unit:MegaPA ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gen:TRF031Shape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:yieldPoint ;
        sh:node gen:StressQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:combinedBendingShearStress ;
        sh:node gen:StressQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:yieldShearStress ;
        sh:node gen:StressQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:beamTheoryUsed ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Combined bending and shear stress must be lower than the yield point." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:yieldPoint ?yieldQuantity ;
                      nltl:combinedBendingShearStress ?combinedQuantity .
                ?yieldQuantity qudt:numericValue ?yield ;
                               qudt:unit ?yieldUnit .
                ?combinedQuantity qudt:numericValue ?combined ;
                                   qudt:unit ?combinedUnit .
                FILTER (?combined >= ?yield)
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Yield shear stress must equal yield point divided by sqrt(3), within a relative tolerance of 1e-6 plus an absolute fallback of 1e-9 MPa." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:yieldPoint ?yieldQuantity ;
                      nltl:yieldShearStress ?resultQuantity .
                ?yieldQuantity qudt:numericValue ?yield ;
                               qudt:unit ?yieldUnit .
                ?resultQuantity qudt:numericValue ?result ;
                                 qudt:unit ?resultUnit .
                BIND (?yield / 1.7320508075688772 AS ?expected)
                BIND ((ABS(?expected) * 0.000001) + 0.000000001 AS ?tolerance)
                FILTER (
                    ?result < (?expected - ?tolerance) ||
                    ?result > (?expected + ?tolerance)
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:beamTheoryUsed ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:allowableShearStress ;
                sh:node gen:StressQuantityShape ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "When beam theory is used, allowable shear stress must be no larger than 0.9 times yield shear stress." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:yieldPoint ?yieldQuantity ;
                              nltl:allowableShearStress ?allowableQuantity .
                        ?yieldQuantity qudt:numericValue ?yield ;
                                       qudt:unit ?yieldUnit .
                        ?allowableQuantity qudt:numericValue ?allowable ;
                                           qudt:unit ?allowableUnit .
                        BIND (?yield / 1.7320508075688772 AS ?yieldShear)
                        FILTER (?allowable > (0.9 * ?yieldShear))
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:beamTheoryUsed ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ) .

```

Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf

Original source text:
Ships of categories A and B, constructed on or after 1 January 2017, shall be able to withstand flooding resulting from hull penetration due to ice impact. The residual stability following ice damage shall be such that the factor s_i, as defined in SOLAS regulations II-1/7-2.2 and II-1/7-2.3, is equal to one for all loading conditions used to calculate the attained subdivision index in SOLAS regulation II-1/7. However, for cargo ships that comply with subdivision and damage stability regulations in another instrument developed by the Organization, as provided by SOLAS regulation II-1/4.1, the residual stability criteria of that instrument shall be met for each loading condition.
Normalized requirement:
IF ship_category IN {'A','B'} AND construction_date >= '2017-01-01', THEN for every applicable loading condition either residual_stability_factor_s_i = 1 or, where the SOLAS II-1/4.1 alternative applies, alternative_instrument_residual_stability_status = compliant.
Required inputs: Ship category, construction date, loading conditions, calculated s_i, and alternative-instrument evidence

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix q: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix qudtu: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-037-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
                sh:in (
                    nltl:polarShipCategoryA
                    nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                ) ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:minInclusive "2017-01-01"^^xsd:date
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasLoadingConditionCase ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class nltl:loadingConditionCase ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:loadingConditionIdentifier ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
                        sh:minCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:hasLoadingConditionResultEvidence ;
                        sh:class nltl:evidenceArtifact
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:alternativeInstrumentApplicable ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:or (
                        [
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path nltl:residualStabilityFactorSI ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                                sh:node [
                                    sh:class q:QuantityValue ;
                                    sh:property [
                                        sh:path q:numericValue ;
                                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                                        sh:minInclusive 1 ;
                                        sh:maxInclusive 1
                                    ] ;
                                    sh:property [
                                        sh:path q:unit ;
                                        sh:hasValue qudtu:UNITLESS ;
                                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                                        sh:maxCount 1
                                    ]
                                ]
                            ]
                        ]
                        [
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path nltl:alternativeInstrumentApplicable ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                                sh:hasValue true ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ] ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path nltl:alternativeInstrumentResidualStabilityStatus ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                                sh:hasValue true ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ]
                        ]
                    ) ;
                    sh:message "Each applicable loading condition must provide either residual stability factor s_i equal to one or a compliant permitted alternative-instrument outcome."
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "Ship category and construction date must be present and must establish that the conditional residual-stability rule is not applicable." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        OPTIONAL { $this nltl:shipCategory ?category . }
                        OPTIONAL { $this nltl:constructionDate ?date . }
                        FILTER (
                            !BOUND(?category) ||
                            !BOUND(?date) ||
                            ?category NOT IN (
                                nltl:polarShipCategoryA,
                                nltl:polarShipCategoryB,
                                nltl:polarShipCategoryC
                            ) ||
                            (
                                ?category IN (
                                    nltl:polarShipCategoryA,
                                    nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                                ) &&
                                ?date >= "2017-01-01"^^xsd:date
                            )
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:message "For category A or B ships constructed on or after 1 January 2017, every loading condition requires the declared residual-stability outcome." .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-037-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:polarShipCategoryA
            nltl:polarShipCategoryB
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship category of Category A or Category B is required to establish applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A construction date is required to establish applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasLoadingConditionCase ;
        sh:class nltl:loadingConditionCase
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For every applicable loading condition, the loading-condition identifier and result evidence must be present; either the residual stability factor must equal 1, or the permitted alternative instrument must be applicable and its residual stability status must be compliant." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this ?case
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:shipCategory ?category ;
                      nltl:constructionDate ?constructionDate ;
                      nltl:hasLoadingConditionCase ?case .

                ?case a nltl:loadingConditionCase .

                FILTER (
                    ?category IN (
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryA,
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                    )
                )
                FILTER (
                    ?constructionDate >= "2017-01-01"^^xsd:date
                )

                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        ?case nltl:loadingConditionIdentifier ?identifier .
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        ?case nltl:hasLoadingConditionResultEvidence ?evidence .
                        ?evidence a nltl:evidenceArtifact .
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        ?case nltl:alternativeInstrumentApplicable ?applicable .
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        ?case nltl:alternativeInstrumentApplicable ?invalidApplicable .
                        FILTER ( datatype(?invalidApplicable) != xsd:boolean )
                    }
                    ||
                    (
                        EXISTS {
                            ?case nltl:alternativeInstrumentApplicable ?alternativeApplicable .
                            FILTER (
                                datatype(?alternativeApplicable) = xsd:boolean &&
                                ?alternativeApplicable = true
                            )
                        }
                        &&
                        NOT EXISTS {
                            ?case nltl:alternativeInstrumentResidualStabilityStatus ?status .
                            FILTER (
                                datatype(?status) = xsd:boolean &&
                                ?status = true
                            )
                        }
                    )
                    ||
                    (
                        NOT EXISTS {
                            ?case nltl:alternativeInstrumentApplicable ?alternativeApplicableFalse .
                            FILTER (
                                datatype(?alternativeApplicableFalse) = xsd:boolean &&
                                ?alternativeApplicableFalse = true
                            )
                        }
                        &&
                        NOT EXISTS {
                            ?case nltl:residualStabilityFactorSI ?factor .
                            ?factor qudt:numericValue ?numericValue ;
                                   qudt:unit qudt:UNITLESS .
                            FILTER (
                                datatype(?numericValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                                ?numericValue = 1
                            )
                        }
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gen:TRF123ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasOccasionalForceLoadCase ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:occasionalForceLoadCase ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerBladeExcludedFromOccasionalForceScope ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "The occasional-force load case must confirm that propeller blades are excluded."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:componentYieldSafetyFactor ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS>
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:occasionalForceCaseAssessedComponent ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:class nltl:shipComponent ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:occasionalForceComponentStress ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ] ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA>
                            ]
                        ]
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:hasMaterialProperties ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:class nltl:materialProperties ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path nltl:componentMaterialYieldStrength ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                                sh:node [
                                    sh:property [
                                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                                        sh:maxCount 1
                                    ] ;
                                    sh:property [
                                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA>
                                    ]
                                ]
                            ]
                        ]
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The externally calculated occasional-force component stress, including the stated safety factor, must not exceed the component material yield strength." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasOccasionalForceLoadCase ?loadCase .
                ?loadCase nltl:propellerBladeExcludedFromOccasionalForceScope true ;
                          nltl:componentYieldSafetyFactor ?factor ;
                          nltl:occasionalForceCaseAssessedComponent ?component .
                ?component nltl:occasionalForceComponentStress ?stress ;
                           nltl:hasMaterialProperties ?properties .
                ?properties nltl:componentMaterialYieldStrength ?yield .
                ?factor qudt:numericValue ?factorValue .
                ?stress qudt:numericValue ?stressValue .
                ?yield qudt:numericValue ?yieldValue .
                FILTER ( ?stressValue * ?factorValue > ?yieldValue )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-123-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasOccasionalForceLoadCase ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:occasionalForceLoadCase ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerBladeExcludedFromOccasionalForceScope ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The occasional force load case must document that propeller blades are excluded from its scope."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:componentYieldSafetyFactor ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:occasionalForceCaseAssessedComponent ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class nltl:shipComponent ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:occasionalForceComponentStress ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ] ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA> ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ]
                        ]
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:hasMaterialProperties ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:class nltl:materialProperties ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path nltl:componentMaterialYieldStrength ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                                sh:node [
                                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                                    sh:property [
                                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                                        sh:maxCount 1
                                    ] ;
                                    sh:property [
                                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA> ;
                                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                                        sh:maxCount 1
                                    ]
                                ]
                            ]
                        ]
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "The externally calculated occasional-force component stress, including the safety factor, must not exceed the component material yield strength." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:occasionalForceCaseAssessedComponent ?component .
                        ?component nltl:hasMaterialProperties ?materialProperties .
                        $this nltl:occasionalForceComponentStress ?stressQuantity .
                        ?stressQuantity qudt:numericValue ?stress .
                        $this nltl:componentYieldSafetyFactor ?factorQuantity .
                        ?factorQuantity qudt:numericValue ?factor .
                        ?materialProperties nltl:componentMaterialYieldStrength ?yieldQuantity .
                        ?yieldQuantity qudt:numericValue ?yield .
                        FILTER (?stress * ?factor > ?yield)
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
    ] .
```
